I found the problems by reading the rendering code instead, checking each suspect object against the same kind of object that the game draws correctly. The project compiles and the existing tests pass, but I haven't run the game to compare against the clip, so please check these against your recording.\n\n**Things a vehicle carries or has carried**\n- **Missile waiting on a vehicle** (`bonus/Missile.java`): it was always drawn on the top (flying) layer. That's why it showed through bridges and other things that hide the vehicle. It also kept its normal colours while the vehicle darkened as it sank into water, and its shadow stayed visible while the vehicle's didn't. It's now drawn on the vehicle's own layer, with the vehicle's tint, and its shadow follows the vehicle's: hidden while falling, offset by height while being lifted. Once fired it's drawn on top as before.\n- **Fired missile's engine flame**: it was drawn beside the missile instead of at its tail, because it was offset along the wrong axis. It now sits at the back, the same way a vehicle's turbo flame does.\n- **Mine carried behind a vehicle** (`bonus/Mine.java`): it stayed on the ground layer even when the vehicle was lifted into the air, and its shadow stayed visible while the vehicle fell. It now moves to the vehicle's layer and its shadow follows the vehicle's. Dropped mines are unchanged.\n\n**Shadows**\n- `utils/BodyRegionDrawer.java`: shadows drawn by this helper were given the angle in radians where degrees were expected, so they barely turned with their object. That affects obstacles, bonus spots, mines and missiles. They now turn with the object.\n- `racer/VehicleRenderer.java`: vehicle shadows fell up and to the left, while every other shadow (helicopter, obstacles) falls down and to the right. They now fall the same way as the others.\n\n**Race camera** (`racescreen/RacerCameraUpdater.java`)\n- The camera shifts ahead of the vehicle it follows, but that shift was mirrored. It was only right when the vehicle drove straight up the screen; heading left or right, the view showed more road behind than ahead. It now always looks ahead.\n- While there, I also fixed the camera's speed limit, which was being applied after the new position had already been set and so did nothing.\n\nTrack layout and vehicle movement are untouched."}],"container":null,"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":2,"cache_creation_input_tokens":372,"cache_read_input_tokens":58989,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":372,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":5,"speed":"standard"},"input_transformations":[],"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"3575f997-969e-448d-9a86-2cec2a39d94d","uuid":"1203c8a3-6df4-4b5c-8ba4-d1b44385151b","timestamp":"2026-11-18T06:53:40.070Z"}

It's now drawn on the vehicle's own layer, with the vehicle's tint, and its shadow follows the vehicle's: hidden while falling, offset by height while being lifted. It now moves to the vehicle's layer and its shadow follows the vehicle's. Dropped mines are unchanged.\n\n**Shadows**\n- `utils/BodyRegionDrawer.java`: shadows drawn by this helper were given the angle in radians where degrees were expected, so they barely turned with their object. That affects obstacles, bonus spots, mines and missiles. They now turn with the object.\n- `racer/VehicleRenderer.java`: vehicle shadows fell up and to the left, while every other shadow (helicopter, obstacles) falls down and to the right. They now fall the same way as the others.\n\n**Race camera** (`racescreen/RacerCameraUpdater.java`)\n- The camera shifts ahead of the vehicle it follows, but that shift was mirrored. It was only right when the vehicle drove straight up the screen; heading left or right, the view showed more road behind than ahead.
